# LeafAlert — Training Data Attribution & Licensing NOTICE

This file is generated by `scripts/generate_attribution.py`. Regenerate it after any dataset fetch.

## ⚠️ NonCommercial constraint

**Part of this training set is licensed CC BY-NC (NonCommercial). The resulting model must not be used commercially unless those images are removed and the model retrained.** If you need a commercially usable model, re-fetch with `--license cc0,cc-by` only and retrain.

## Summary

- Training pool: **9391** images across poison_ivy, poison_oak, poison_sumac, safe_plants.
- With recorded provenance: **7954** images from **3229** source observations by **3418** photographers.
- ⚠️ **1437** pre-expansion images have **no recorded license or attribution** (they predate the provenance-tracking fetchers). Their licensing is UNVERIFIED — audit or replace them before any public distribution.

License breakdown of the provenance-tracked images:

| License | Images | Commercial use |
|---|---|---|
| CC BY-NC 4.0 (NonCommercial) | 5000 | no |
| CC BY 4.0 | 1207 | yes |
| CC BY 4.0 | 806 | yes |
| CC0 1.0 (public domain dedication) | 610 | yes |
| CC0 1.0 (public domain dedication) | 331 | yes |

Per-class (provenance-tracked): poison_ivy 2546, poison_oak 2087, poison_sumac 895, safe_plants 2426

## Source

Images were retrieved from [iNaturalist](https://www.inaturalist.org) (research-grade observations) under the licenses above. Each credit below links to the source observation, which carries the authoritative license and photographer information.
